[USER]

[GAME] Welcome to Secret Mafia! You are Player 4.
Your role: Villager
Team: Village
Description: A regular villager. Your goal is to identify and eliminate all Mafia members through voting during the day.

Players: Player 0, Player 1, Player 2, Player 3, Player 4, Player 5, Player 6, Player 7

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

[GAME] No one was killed tonight.
[GAME] Day breaks. Discuss for 3 rounds, then a vote will follow.

[COMPLETION]
**Player 4 (Villager):** Alright, everyone, let's start discussing who we think might be the Mafia. I haven't noticed anyone acting suspiciously yet, but I'm sure there's someone among us. Let's go around and share our thoughts. Who do you think is the most likely to be the Mafia?

[ACTION=**Player 4 (Villager):** Alright, everyone, let's start discussing who we think might be the Mafia. I haven't noticed anyone acting suspiciously yet, but I'm sure there's someone among us. Let's go around and share our thoughts. Who do you think is the most likely to be the Mafia?  REWARD=0.000  RETRIES=0]

- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

[GAME] No one was killed tonight.

Description: A regular villager. Your goal is to identify and eliminate all Mafia members through voting during the day.

Players: Player 0, Player 1, Player 2, Player 3, Player 4, Player 5, Player 6, Player 7

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

Description: A regular villager. Your goal is to identify and eliminate all Mafia members through voting during the day.

Players: Player 0, Player 1, Player 2, Player 3, Player 4, Player 5, Player 6, Player 7

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

[GAME] Welcome to Secret Mafia! You are Player 1.
Your role: Villager
Team: Village
Description: A regular villager. Your goal is to identify and eliminate all Mafia members through voting during the day.

Players: Player 0, Player 1, Player 2, Player 3, Player 4, Player 5, Player 6, Player 7

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

Team: Village
Description: A regular villager. Your goal is to identify and eliminate all Mafia members through voting during the day.

Players: Player 0, Player 1, Player 2, Player 3, Player 4, Player 5, Player 6, Player 7

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

Your role: Villager
Team: Village
Description: A regular villager. Your goal is to identify and eliminate all Mafia members through voting during the day.

Players: Player 0, Player 1, Player 2, Player 3, Player 4, Player 5, Player 6, Player 7

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).
